```
44	function cloneGs(gs: GenState): GenState {

48	    if (Array.isArray(v)) c[k] = v.map((e) => (e && typeof e === 'object' ? { ...(e as object) } : e));
49	    else if (v instanceof Int32Array) c[k] = v.slice();
50	    else if (v instanceof Float32Array) c[k] = v.slice();
51	    else if (v instanceof Uint8Array) c[k] = v.slice();

61	  const box = { n: 0 };
62	  const rolls: Array<[number, string, number, number, number]> = [];
63	  ur.internalSample = () => { box.n++; return orig(); };
64	  // RNG 门面日志：int/next 逐调用记 (k, 调用形, lo, hi, val)

66	  if (g.__swRollLog) {

69	      (rng[key] as unknown as (...a: number[]) => number) = (...a: number[]) => {

71	        g.__swRollLog!.push([box.n, key, a[0] ?? 0, a[1] ?? 0, v]);
72	        (globalThis as { __swRollN?: number }).__swRollN = box.n;
73	        return v;
74	      };
75	    }
76	  }
77	  return { rng, box };

91	    hLty = new Uint8Array(readFileSync('/tmp/hg/chestd59v2.lty'));
92	    hWire = new Uint8Array(readFileSync('/tmp/hg/chestd59v2.wire'));
93	  } catch { /* 缺真值回退管线捕获 */ }
94	  const st = new TileStore(W, H);
95	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {

97	    st.flags[i] = a[i] ? 1 : 0;
98	    st.wall[i] = w[i]!;
99	    st.liquid[i] = l[i]!;
100	    st.liquidType[i] = hLty ? hLty[i] : cap.aux.liquidType[i];       // D59 已按本仓 +1 编码（liquid>0 ? lty+1 : 0）
101	    st.half[i] = hHalf ? hHalf[i] : cap.aux.half[i];
102	    st.slope[i] = hSlope ? hSlope[i] : cap.aux.slope[i];
103	    st.wire[i] = hWire ? hWire[i] : cap.aux.wire[i];
104	  }
105	  return st;
106	}

108	interface DiffRes { typ: number; act: number; wal: number; liq: number; pairs: [string, number][]; samples: string[]; mask: Uint8Array }
109	function diff(st: TileStore, idx: number, label: string, prevMask?: Uint8Array): DiffRes {
110	  const t = loadT(idx), a = loadA(idx), w = loadW(idx), l = loadL(idx);
111	  let typ = 0, act = 0, wal = 0, liq = 0;
112	  const pairs = new Map<string, number>();
113	  const samples: string[] = [];
114	  const mask = new Uint8Array(N);
115	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
116	    let jT = st.type[i]; if (jT !== 0) jT = SHEET[jT] ?? -1;
117	    const dT = jT !== t[i], dA = (st.flags[i] !== 0) !== (a[i] !== 0), dW = st.wall[i] !== w[i], dL = st.liquid[i] !== l[i];
118	    if (!(dT || dA || dW || dL)) continue;
119	    mask[i] = 1;
120	    if (dT) { typ++; pairs.set(`t${jT}>${t[i]}`, (pairs.get(`t${jT}>${t[i]}`) ?? 0) + 1); }
121	    if (dA) { act++; pairs.set(`a${st.flags[i] ? 1 : 0}>${a[i]}`, (pairs.get(`a${st.flags[i] ? 1 : 0}>${a[i]}`) ?? 0) + 1); }
122	    if (dW) { wal++; pairs.set(`W${st.wall[i]}>${w[i]}`, (pairs.get(`W${st.wall[i]}>${w[i]}`) ?? 0) + 1); }
123	    if (dL) liq++;
124	    if (samples.length < 8) samples.push(`(${i % W},${(i / W) | 0})t${jT}>${t[i]}a${st.flags[i] ? 1 : 0}>${a[i]}w${st.wall[i]}>${w[i]}L${st.liquid[i]}>${l[i]}`);
125	  }
126	  const top = [...pairs.entries()].sort((p, q) => q[1] - p[1]).slice(0, 14);
127	  console.log(`${label}: typ=${typ} act=${act} wal=${wal} liq=${liq}`);
128	  console.log('  pairs:', JSON.stringify(top));
129	  console.log('  samples:', samples.join(' | '));
130	  if (prevMask) {
131	    let nt = 0, na = 0, nw = 0, nl = 0;
132	    for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
133	      if (!mask[i] || prevMask[i]) continue;
134	      let jT = st.type[i]; if (jT !== 0) jT = SHEET[jT] ?? -1;
135	      if (jT !== t[i]) nt++;
136	      if ((st.flags[i] !== 0) !== (a[i] !== 0)) na++;
137	      if (st.wall[i] !== w[i]) nw++;
138	      if (st.liquid[i] !== l[i]) nl++;
139	    }
140	    console.log(`  new(相对prevMask): typ=${nt} act=${na} wal=${nw} liq=${nl}`);
141	  }
142	  return { typ, act, wal, liq, pairs: top, samples, mask };
143	}
144	
145	/** 足迹对拍：vanilla 改动集（in→out dump）与 JS 改动集（in dump→JS 出）的分渠道计数 + 首/尾分歧点 */
146	function footprints(st: TileStore, inIdx: number, outIdx: number) {
147	  const ti = loadT(inIdx), ai = loadA(inIdx), wi = loadW(inIdx), li = loadL(inIdx);
148	  const to = loadT(outIdx), ao = loadA(outIdx), wo = loadW(outIdx), lo = loadL(outIdx);
149	  const vanT: number[] = [], vanW: number[] = [], jsT: number[] = [], jsW: number[] = [];
150	  const vanList: Array<[number, number, string]> = [], jsList: Array<[number, number, string]> = [];
151	  for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
152	    const x = i % W, y = (i / W) | 0;
153	    if (ti[i] !== to[i] || (ai[i] !== 0) !== (ao[i] !== 0) || wi[i] !== wo[i]) {
154	      vanList.push([x, y, `t${ti[i]}${ai[i] ? '*' : '.'}>${to[i]}${ao[i] ? '*' : '.'}w${wi[i]}>${wo[i]}`]);
155	      if (ti[i] !== to[i] || (ai[i] !== 0) !== (ao[i] !== 0)) vanT.push(i);
156	      if (wi[i] !== wo[i]) vanW.push(i);
157	    }
158	    let jT = st.type[i]; if (jT !== 0) jT = SHEET[jT] ?? -1;
159	    const jA = st.flags[i] !== 0;
160	    if (jT !== ti[i] || jA !== (ai[i] !== 0) || st.wall[i] !== wi[i]) {
161	      jsList.push([x, y, `t${ti[i]}${ai[i] ? '*' : '.'}>${jT}${jA ? '*' : '.'}w${wi[i]}>${st.wall[i]}`]);
162	      if (jT !== ti[i] || jA !== (ai[i] !== 0)) jsT.push(i);
163	      if (st.wall[i] !== wi[i]) jsW.push(i);
164	    }
165	  }
166	  console.log(`  足迹: vanilla typ/act格=${vanT.length} wal格=${vanW.length} | JS typ/act格=${jsT.length} wal格=${jsW.length}`);
167	  const vanSet = new Set(vanList.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`));
168	  const jsSet = new Set(jsList.map(([x, y]) => `${x},${y}`));
169	  const onlyVan = vanList.filter(([x, y]) => !jsSet.has(`${x},${y}`));
170	  const onlyJs = jsList.filter(([x, y]) => !vanSet.has(`${x},${y}`));
171	  console.log(`  vanilla-only=${onlyVan.length} (前6: ${onlyVan.slice(0, 6).map((e) => e.join(',')).join(' ')})  JS-only=${onlyJs.length} (前6: ${onlyJs.slice(0, 6).map((e) => e.join(',')).join(' ')})`);
172	  // 首个 onlyJs 事件的 7×7 邻域三视图
173	  if (onlyJs.length) {
174	    const [bx, by] = onlyJs[0]!;
175	    const rows: string[] = [];
176	    for (let dy = -3; dy <= 3; dy++) {
177	      let rI = '', rV = '', rJ = '';
178	      for (let dx = -3; dx <= 3; dx++) {
179	        const i = (by + dy) * W + bx + dx;
180	        rI += `${ti[i] ?? '?'}${ai[i] ? '*' : '.'} `;
181	        rV += `${to[i] ?? '?'}${ao[i] ? '*' : '.'} `;
182	        let jT = st.type[i]; if (jT !== 0) jT = SHEET[jT] ?? -1;
183	        rJ += `${jT}${st.flags[i] ? '*' : '.'} `;
184	      }
185	      rows.push(`y${by + dy}: in[${rI}] van[${rV}] js[${rJ}]`);
186	    }
187	    console.log(`  首JS-only(${bx},${by}) 7×7:\n    ` + rows.join('\n    '));
188	    const near = onlyJs.slice(0, 12).map(([x, y, s]) => `(${x},${y})${s}`);
189	    console.log(`  JS-only 前12: ${near.join(' ')}`);
190	    const nearV = onlyVan.slice(0, 12).map(([x, y, s]) => `(${x},${y})${s}`);
191	    console.log(`  van-only 前12: ${nearV.join(' ')}`);
192	  }
193	}
194	
195	(RUN ? it : it.skip)('chest-family isolated replay', async () => {
196	  // ── A) 真管线捕获 ──
197	  const origReseed = RNG.prototype.reseed;
198	  const WANT = new Set(['UndergroundHousesAndBuriedChests', 'SurfaceChests', 'UnderwaterChests', 'SpiderCaves']);
199	  let store: TileStore | null = null;
200	  const onReseed = function (this: RNG, seed: number) {
201	    origReseed.call(this, seed);
202	    try {
203	      const stackLine = (new Error().stack ?? '').split('\n').find((l) => l.includes('/src/') && !l.includes('rng.ts')) ?? '?';
204	      const mLoc = /\(.*(src\/[^\s?]+):(\d+):\d+\)/.exec(stackLine);
205	      if (!mLoc || !mLoc[1]!.includes('WorldGen.ts') || !store) return;
206	      const abs = `~/Project/GLM/SandboxWorld/game/${mLoc[1]!.slice(mLoc[1]!.indexOf('src/'))}`;
207	      const src = readFileSync(abs, 'utf8').split('\n');
208	      for (let k = +mLoc[2]; k >= Math.max(0, +mLoc[2] - 2); k--) {
209	        const cm = /\/\/\s*▶([^\s(（]+)/.exec(src[k - 1] ?? '');
210	        if (cm && WANT.has(cm[1]!) && !caps[cm[1]!]) {
211	          const gs = lastGenState();
212	          if (!gs) return;
213	          caps[cm[1]!] = {
214	            gs: cloneGs(gs), solid: snapshotGenSolid(),
215	            aux: { liquidType: store.liquidType.slice(), half: store.half.slice(), slope: store.slope.slice(), wire: store.wire.slice() },
216	          };
217	          console.log(`captured @▶${cm[1]}`);
218	        }
219	      }
220	    } catch { /* probe */ }
221	  };
222	  (RNG.prototype as unknown as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = onReseed;
223	  try {
224	    await generateWorld({ width: W, height: H, seedText: '9293480', evil: 0, onWorldPartial: (w) => { store = w.store; } });
225	  } catch (e) {
226	    console.log('pipeline EX(忽略):', String(e).slice(0, 200));
227	  } finally {
228	    (RNG.prototype as unknown as { reseed: unknown }).reseed = origReseed;
229	  }
230	  console.log('caps:', Object.keys(caps).join(','));
231	
232	  // ── B) #58 窗：dump058 → #58 链 → dump059 ──
233	  {
234	    const cap = caps.UndergroundHousesAndBuriedChests!;
235	    const st = buildStore(58, cap);
236	    restoreGenSolid(cap.solid);
237	    const gs = cloneGs(cap.gs);
238	    const chests: ChestSink = [];
239	    (globalThis as { __swRollLog?: unknown }).__swRollLog = [];
240	    const houseEv: Array<[string, number, number, number]> = [];
241	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swHouseEv?: (p: string, x: number, y: number) => void }).__swHouseEv
242	      = (p, x, y) => { houseEv.push([p, x, y, (globalThis as { __swRollN?: number }).__swRollN ?? -1]); };
243	    const chestEv: Array<[string, number, number, number]> = [];
244	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swChestEv?: (p: string, i: number, j: number, x?: number) => void }).__swChestEv
245	      = (p, i, j, x) => { chestEv.push([p, i, j, x ?? (globalThis as { __swRollN?: number }).__swRollN ?? -1]); };
246	    const stageEv: Array<[string, number, number, number, number]> = [];
247	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swHouseStage?: (tag: string, a: number, b: number, c: number, d: number) => void }).__swHouseStage
248	      = (tag, a, b, c, d) => { stageEv.push([tag, a, b, c, d]); };
249	    const { rng, box } = countedRng(gs.seed);
250	    flipGenSolid([226, 162, 225, 190], true);
251	    rollHouseChestCounts(st, rng, gs);
252	    const counts = { house: gs.caveHouseCount, uw: gs.underworldChestCount, cave: gs.caveChestCount, desert: gs.desertHouseCount };
253	    runBuriedChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
254	    runUnderworldChestsPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
255	    runCaveHousesPass(st, rng, gs, chests);
256	    flipGenSolid([226, 162, 225, 190], false);
257	    console.log(`\n[#58 重放] draws=${box.n} counts=${JSON.stringify(counts)} chests=${chests.length}`);
258	    // 落盘 JS 重放输出 + chests 清单供 python 分析（van N 定标：P59=76364 P60=28810 P62=5495 P63=160186）
259	    try {
260	      const fsmod = await import('node:fs');
261	      fsmod.mkdirSync('/tmp/sw-chest', { recursive: true });
262	      const wf = fsmod.writeFileSync;
263	      const bt = Buffer.alloc(N * 2), bw = Buffer.alloc(N * 2);
264	      for (let i = 0; i < N; i++) {
265	        let jT = st.type[i]; if (jT !== 0) jT = SHEET[jT] ?? -1;
266	        bt.writeUInt16LE(jT & 0xffff, i * 2);
267	        bw.writeUInt16LE(st.wall[i] & 0xffff, i * 2);
268	      }
269	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.typ', bt); wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.act', Buffer.from(Uint8Array.from(st.flags, (f) => f & 1)));
270	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.wal', bw); wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.liq', Buffer.from(st.liquid.slice()));
271	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.chests.json', JSON.stringify(chests.map((c) => ({ x: c.x, y: c.y, items: c.items }))));
272	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.rolls.json', JSON.stringify((globalThis as { __swRollLog?: Array<[number, string, number, number, number]> }).__swRollLog ?? []));
273	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.houses.json', JSON.stringify(houseEv));
274	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.chestev.json', JSON.stringify(chestEv));
275	      wf('/tmp/sw-chest/js59.stageev.json', JSON.stringify(stageEv));
276	    } catch (e) { console.log('save fail', String(e).slice(0, 120)); }
277	    (globalThis as { __swRollLog?: unknown }).__swRollLog = undefined;
278	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swHouseEv?: unknown }).__swHouseEv = undefined;
279	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swChestEv?: unknown }).__swChestEv = undefined;
280	    (globalThis as unknown as { __swHouseStage?: unknown }).__swHouseStage = undefined;

284	    {
285	      const ti = loadT(58), ai = loadA(58), to = loadT(59), ao = loadA(59);
286	      const vanChests: Array<[number, number, number]> = [];

294	        }

296	      const jsList = chests.map((c) => [c.x, c.y, SHEET[st.type[c.y * W + c.x]] ?? -1] as [number, number, number])
297	        .sort((a, b) => (a[1] - b[1]) || (a[0] - b[0]));
298	      const vanList = vanChests.sort((a, b) => (a[1] - b[1]) || (a[0] - b[0]));
299	      console.log(`  宝箱清单: vanilla=${vanList.length} JS=${jsList.length}`);
300	      const key = (c: [number, number, number]) => `${c[0]},${c[1]}`;
301	      const vanKeys = new Set(vanList.map(key));
302	      const jsKeys = new Set(jsList.map(key));
303	      let firstBad = -1;
304	      const merged: Array<[number, number, number, string]> = [];
305	      for (const c of vanList) merged.push([c[0], c[1], c[2], jsKeys.has(key(c)) ? '=' : 'V-only']);
306	      for (const c of jsList) if (!vanKeys.has(key(c))) merged.push([c[0], c[1], c[2], 'J-only']);
307	      merged.sort((a, b) => (a[1] - b[1]) || (a[0] - b[0]));
308	      for (let k = 0; k < merged.length; k++) if (merged[k]![3] !== '=') { firstBad = k; break; }
309	      console.log(`  合并序前 40（cell 序）: ${merged.slice(0, 40).map((m) => `(${m[0]},${m[1]})t${m[2]}${m[3] === '=' ? '' : m[3]}`).join(' ')}`);
310	      console.log(`  首分歧序=${firstBad} → ${firstBad >= 0 ? merged[firstBad]!.join(',') : '全同'}`);
311	      // 流序对拍：chests[] 前 54 = 循环①(39)+②(15)，其后=小屋序。逐个标 ∈vanilla?
312	      const streamTag = chests.map((c, idx) => {

314	        return `${idx}${tag}${vanKeys.has(`${c.x},${c.y}`) ? '=' : '×'}(${c.x},${c.y})`;

321	      console.log(`  未命中: 循环① ${missC}/39 循环② ${missU}/15 小屋 ${missH}/${Math.max(0, streamTag.length - 54)}`);
```

```
using Mono.Cecil;
using Mono.Cecil.Cil;
var exe = "/tmp/sw-num4/backup/TerrariaServer.exe.orig";
var resolver = new DefaultAssemblyResolver();
resolver.AddSearchDirectory(Path.GetDirectoryName(exe)!);
var asm = AssemblyDefinition.ReadAssembly(exe, new ReaderParameters { AssemblyResolver = resolver, ReadSymbols = false });
var mod = asm.MainModule;
var wg = mod.Types.First(t => t.FullName == "Terraria.WorldGen");
var ab = wg.Methods.First(m => m.Name == "AddBuriedChest" && m.Parameters.Count == 8);
var il = ab.Body.Instructions;
for (int i = 0; i < il.Count; i++)
{
    if (il[i].Offset == 0x33DE)
    {
        for (int j = Math.Max(0, i - 40); j <= i + 10; j++)
        {
            var x = il[j];
            string op = x.Operand is MethodReference m2 ? m2.DeclaringType.Name + "::" + m2.Name + "(" + m2.Parameters.Count + ")" : x.Operand is FieldReference f ? f.DeclaringType.Name + "::" + f.Name : x.Operand?.ToString() ?? "";
            Console.WriteLine($"  IL_{x.Offset:X4} {x.OpCode,-12} {op}   -> {(x.Operand is Instruction t2 ? "IL_" + t2.Offset.ToString("X4") : "")}");
        }
    }
}
```

````
Now I fully understand the vanilla Prefix re-roll loop:

```csharp
while (flag) {
    flag = false;
    if (rolledPrefix == -1 && unifiedRandom.Next(4) == 0) rolledPrefix = 0;  // Next(4) roll each loop!
    if (prefixWeWant < -1) rolledPrefix = -1;
    if ((rolledPrefix == -1 || -2 || -3) && !RollAPrefix(unifiedRandom, ref rolledPrefix)) return false;  // Next(pool) roll
    if (prefixWeWant == -1 && PrefixID.Sets.ReducedNaturalChance[rolledPrefix] && unifiedRandom.Next(3) != 0) rolledPrefix = 0;  // Next(3) roll
    if (prefixWeWant == -4) rolledPrefix = 0;
    if (!TryGetPrefixStatMultipliersForItem(rolledPrefix, out ...)) {
        flag = true;
        rolledPrefix = -1;   // re-enter loop → Next(4) again + RollAPrefix again
    }
    if (prefixWeWant == -2 && rolledPrefix == 0) { rolledPrefix = -1; flag = true; }
}
```

Failure types (return false from TryGet):
- F1: `dmg != 1f && Math.Round((float)damage * dmg) == (double)damage` — damage multiplication produces no change (banker's rounding)
- F2: `spd != 1f && Math.Round((float)useAnimation * spd) == (double)useAnimation` — speed no change
- F3: `mcst != 1f && Math.Round((float)mana * mcst) == (double)mana` — mana no change
- F4: `kb != 1f && knockBack == 0f` — knockBack is zero but prefix wants to modify kb

When failure → loop again: Next(4) roll (if rolledPrefix==-1 → prefixWeWant==-1 here, yes since Prefix(-1) is used) → 25% chance rolledPrefix=0 → then TryGet(0): prefix 0 → all multipliers 1 → passes → loop exits with rolledPrefix=0 (no prefix). 75% → RollAPrefix Next(pool) → check ReducedNaturalChance Next(3) → TryGet again...

So for each re-roll iteration, the roll consumption is:
- Next(4) (1 sample)
- if 0: no further rolls; TryGet(0) succeeds (rolledPrefix=0 → all default → no failure) → exit loop.
- if != 0: RollAPrefix → Next(poolLength) (1 sample); if rolled prefix ∈ ReducedNaturalChance && Next(3)!=0 → rolledPrefix=0 → TryGet(0) OK; else TryGet(rolled) may fail again → repeat.

Wait, careful — order: Next(4) first, then RollAPrefix Next(pool), then possibly Next(3), then TryGet.

The first iteration of the loop is already handled by the existing implementation presumably (the current JS model does: Next(4) roll — the "Prefix Next(4) 早退" from the report — then pool roll, then ReducedNaturalChance Next(3)). What's missing is: if TryGet fails on the rolled prefix, loop back → more Next(4)+pool(+Next(3)) rolls.

So I need per-item tables:
- damage, useAnimation, mana, knockBack for each chest-loot item that can be prefixed (only items whose pools exist — i.e., the items in the PRE_CLASS 主件类表 + others).
- The prefix multiplier table (from TryGetPrefixStatMultipliersForItem above — this is fixed data, all multipliers).
- The rollable prefixes pools per class (PrefixesForSwords etc.) — presumably already in the JS implementation (since pool rolls consume 1 sample each; the existing code models pool rolls? Let me check BuriedChestsPass).

- ReducedNaturalChance set — which prefixes are reduced.
- Banker's rounding: Math.Round(double) with MidpointRounding.ToEven.

Also `Math.Round((float)damage * dmg)` — note: float*float = float, then Math.Round(float→double). Actually Math.Round((float)damage * dmg) — damage is int, cast to float, times dmg (float) = float; Math.Round(float) — there is Math.Round(double); float implicitly converts to double. The float arithmetic: e.g. damage=10, dmg=1.1 → 10f*1.1f = 11.000001 (float) → Round → 11 ≠ 10 → no failure. For failure, need round(damage*dmg) == damage, e.g., damage=0? damage=0: 0*anything=0 → round=0==0 → FAIL (any dmg prefix fails!). damage=1 with dmg=1.05: 1.05 → round=1 == 1 → FAIL. So low-damage items fail dmg prefixes.

IMPORTANT: must emulate float32 arithmetic exactly (JS Math.fround) and then banker's rounding on the double.

Also for spd: useAnimation * spd == useAnimation after rounding. useAnimation=0? or 1? e.g. useAnimation=10, spd=1.1 → 11 → ≠10 OK. useAnimation=0 → 0 → ==0 → FAIL. useAnimation=5, spd=0.9 → 4.5 → banker's → 4 ≠ 5 OK. Actually with float arithmetic 5f*0.9f = 4.5f exactly? 0.9f = 0.89999997615814208984375; 5*that = 4.49999988079071044921875 → rounds to 4 → ≠5 OK.

Hmm — but which items in the chest loot pools can even be prefixed and have failing stats? The four observed: hell tail 3-4 rolls × 4/15 chests. The four failing types: e.g., an item with knockBack==0 getting a kb prefix (F4); low damage items (F1); etc.
````

## 🤖 Assistant · 2026-08-17T02:40:37.803Z · glm-x-preview-260804

<details open>
<summary>💭 thinking</summary>

````
Now I see the current `prefixRolls`. The missing part: the TryGet failure re-roll loop. Current model:
- Next(4): if != 0 → pool roll → ReducedNaturalChance → Next(3) (discard).
- Missing: if the rolled prefix fails TryGet → loop: Next(4) again, etc.

Now the fix per the report: implement per-item property table (damage/useAnimation/mana/knockBack) + prefix multiplier table + banker's rounding, and re-roll loop 1:1.

The loop in vanilla Prefix(-1):
```
flag=true initially
while(flag):
  flag=false
  if rolledPrefix==-1 and Next(4)==0: rolledPrefix=0
  # prefixWeWant=-1 (not < -1)
  if rolledPrefix in {-1,-2,-3} and !RollAPrefix(): return false  # never fails (pool exists)
  if prefixWeWant==-1 and ReducedNaturalChance[rolledPrefix] and Next(3)!=0: rolledPrefix=0
  if !TryGet(rolledPrefix): flag=true; rolledPrefix=-1
  # prefixWeWant==-1 so second re-loop condition (prefixWeWant==-2 && rolledPrefix==0) false
```

Iteration 1: rolledPrefix=-1 → Next(4); if !=0 → RollAPrefix (Next(pool)); if rolled ∈ Reduced and Next(3)!=0 → rolledPrefix=0. TryGet: 0 → OK (all mult=1 → checks: dmg!=1 fails → pass... all four checks require mult!=1, so prefix 0 always succeeds) → exit.
If TryGet(rolled) fails → flag=true, rolledPrefix=-1 → iteration 2: Next(4) again; if !=0 → pool again; ... until success.

So consumed rolls per prefix attempt: min sequence. The number of iterations is data-dependent (item stats + rolled prefixes from the RNG stream).

So I need:
1. PRE_MULTI: prefix id → {dmg,kb,spd,mcst,(others irrelevant for checks)}. The checks only use dmg, spd, mcst, kb.
2. Per-item stats: damage, useAnimation, mana, knockBack (float) for each prefixable item (the items in PRE_CLASS — about 36+17=53 entries).
3. Banker's rounding on float32 product.

Where to get item stats? The repo has item data extracted (vi_ tables / data/items). There's probably a data file with damage/useAnimation/mana/knockBack per vanilla item id. Let me check src/data/items or similar. Also knockBack is float.

Let me check what item data exists.
````

</details>

````
Implementation of banker's rounding: r = Math.round(v); if |v - r| == 0.5 exactly → pick even. Math.round(-x.5) issues — v is always ≥0 here. So:

```js
function roundHalfEven(v: number): number {
  const fl = Math.floor(v);
  const diff = v - fl;
  if (diff > 0.5) return fl + 1;
  if (diff < 0.5) return fl;
  return (fl % 2 === 0) ? fl : fl + 1;
}
```

  const stats = PRE_STATS[item]; // {damage,useAnimation,mana,knockBack}

  }
}
````

````
The SurfaceChests pass ("蓄水池" = the num8 reservoir-sampling loop! The `genRand.Next(num8)` with num8++ IS reservoir sampling — that's the "蓄水池 num8 演进序"). The floating-island chest search:

```csharp
int num5 = 50;
int num6 = num3, num7 = num4;
int num8 = 1;
for (int j = num6 - num5; j <= num6 + num5; j += 2)
  for (int k = num7 - num5; k <= num7 + num5; k += 2)
    if ((double)k < Main.worldSurface && !Main.tile[j, k].active() && Main.tile[j, k].wall == 244 && genRand.Next(num8) == 0)
    {
      flag2 = true;
      num8++;
      flag3 = true;
      num3 = j; num4 = k;
    }
```

This is the sky-island chest placement: scans a 101×101 area (step 2) around the initial point for empty tiles with wall 244 (sky brick wall? 244 = sky wall), reservoir-sampling candidates: each time a candidate found, `genRand.Next(num8)` (num8 grows 1,2,3...) == 0 → select it (probability 1/num8). So the roll count here varies. This is where "位置半数差" (27/27 vanilla-only / 54 JS-only?) comes from — sky chest positions.

Wait the report says "#60 SurfaceChests 补传 notNearOtherChests:true (cs:17282)：12,904→20,639 掷（N₆₀=28,810 的 72%）；位置 27/27 vanilla-only=54 JS-only=54（半数差）"。Hmm — 27 vanilla chests / 27 vanilla-only positions and 54 JS-only positions. Interesting.

Current JS 20,639 vs vanilla 28,810 → 8,171 missing. If the JS doesn't model the reservoir loop (num8) at all (e.g., picks first candidate without rolls), that could account for thousands of rolls: each pass i (21 total for 4200×0.005=21) with up to 2000 attempts each... Actually how many rolls would the reservoir loop consume? For each attempt that lands on a sky island region (active tile), scan ~51×51=2601 cells (j from -50..+50 step 2 = 51 values, same k) — with candidates (empty + wall 244) each consuming 1 roll. If JS doesn't roll here, big deficit. The missing 8.2k over 21 chests ≈ 390 rolls per chest — plausible for reservoir scans (2601 cells scan, maybe ~100-400 candidates with rolls... plausible).
````

```
818	export function runWaterChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {

831	        case 2: main = 4404; break;
832	        case 3: main = 277; break;
833	        default: main = 187; num4 = 0; break;

835	    }

838	      const yLo = loop === 0 ? 1 : Math.floor(gs.worldSurface);
839	      let ok = false;
840	      for (let tries = 0; tries < 10000 && !ok; tries++) {
841	        let x = rng.int(50, w - 51);                                    // Next(50, w-50)
842	        let y = rng.int(yLo, st.h - 201);                               // Next(?, h-200)
843	        // cs:17425-17428：液体不足或非水 → 重掷（重掷 y 域换 [50, h-200)）。
844	        // liquidType 修 2026-08-12：本仓库 +1 编码(水=LIQUID_TYPE.WATER=1)——照抄原版
845	        // !==0(原版 Water=0) 会让水格恒不命中,拒绝采样死循环(世界生成卡"生物群系"根因)。
846	        // 守卫:100 万掷未命中 → 大声报错降级继续(防同类移植错位再变静默死循环)
847	        let __guard = 0;
848	        while (st.liquid[st.idx(x, y)] < 250 || st.liquidType[st.idx(x, y)] !== LIQUID_TYPE.WATER) {
849	          x = rng.int(50, w - 51);
850	          y = rng.int(50, st.h - 201);
851	          if (++__guard >= 1_000_000) {
852	            console.error('[worldgen] runWaterChestsPass 拒绝采样 100 万掷未命中水中格——疑似 liquidType/liquid 编码错位或本世界无合格水体,降级跳过本箱');
853	            break;
854	          }
855	        }
856	        if (__guard >= 1_000_000) continue;
857	        // cs:17427：trySlope 门 = 落点在海滩带内（x < 380 || x > w-380）
858	        if (placeBuriedChest(st, rng, gs, chests, x, y, {
859	          style: 17, loot: main, flags: { water: true },
860	          trySlope: x < gs.beachDistance || x > w - gs.beachDistance,
861	        })) ok = true;
862	      }
863	    }
864	  }
865	}
```

```
"""FNV(Main.rand state) -> pass-local k（UR(9293480) reseed 后掷数）。"""
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, '/tmp/dgu')
from pyur import UR

SEED = 9293480

def fnv(sa, inext):
    h = 2166136261
    M = 0xFFFFFFFF
    for v in sa:
        h = ((h ^ (v & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
        h = ((h ^ ((v >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
        h = ((h ^ ((v >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
        h = ((h ^ ((v >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
    h = ((h ^ (inext & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
    h = ((h ^ ((inext >> 8) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
    h = ((h ^ ((inext >> 16) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
    h = ((h ^ ((inext >> 24) & 0xFF)) * 0x1000193) & M
    return h

def build(kmax=90000):
    u = UR(SEED)
    table = {}
    for k in range(kmax):
        u.internal_sample()
        table[fnv(u.sa, u.inext)] = k + 1  # k+1 掷后的 hash
    return table

if __name__ == '__main__':
    t = build()
    print('size', len(t))
    # sanity: 对照 chest-seg59.json 首事件 AB k=6 hash 缺失——seg 里无 hash，用 ABX|1|<hash> 对拍
    import json
    # 从原始 chestprobe.txt 拿一段对拍
    try:
        lines = [l.split('|') for l in open('/tmp/hg/houseprobe.txt').read().splitlines() if l.startswith('RP|Buried Chests')]
        print(lines[:1])
    except Exception as e:
        print('probe not ready:', e)
AB|3759|631|0|-1|0|0|k=6
```

````
```ts
const f32 = Math.fround;
function netRoundEq(v32: number, base: number): boolean {
  // v32 already fround'ed product; Math.Round(double, ToEven)
  const fl = Math.floor(v32);
  const diff = v32 - fl;
  let r: number;
  if (diff > 0.5) r = fl + 1;
  else if (diff < 0.5) r = fl;
  else r = (fl % 2 === 0) ? fl : fl + 1;  // exactly .5 → even
  return r === base;
}
```
Check: tryGet fail conditions:
```ts
function tryGetFails(rolled: number, s: Stats): boolean {
  const m = PRE_MULT[rolled]; // undefined → all 1 → pass
  if (m) {
    if (m[0] !== 1 && netRoundEq(f32(f32(s.dmg) * m[0]), s.dmg)) return true;
    if (m[2] !== 1 && netRoundEq(f32(f32(s.ua) * m[2]), s.ua)) return true;
    if (m[3] !== 1 && netRoundEq(f32(f32(s.mana) * m[3]), s.mana)) return true;
    if (m[1] !== 1 && s.kb === 0) return true;
  }
  return false;
}
```

In JS, `1.05` is the double 1.05, NOT 1.05f. I must fround the multiplier: store in table as fround'd values — e.g., PRE_MULT entries computed with Math.fround at module init, or store raw and fround at use: `const mm = f32(m[0])`.

For a*b with a,b float32: the exact product may need >24 bits; IEEE float multiply rounds once to nearest float. Computing in double (exact product fits in 53 bits — for our small ints × ~8-bit mantissa values, exact) then rounding once to float32 = same as direct float multiply ✓ (double rounding hazard: exact→double is exact here since product of two 24-bit mantissas needs ≤48 bits < 53 ✓).

function prefixRolls(rng: RNG, item: number): void {
  const cls = PRE_CLASS[item];
  if (!cls) return;
  const arr = PRE_POOLS[cls]!;
  const st = PRE_STATS[item];
  let rolled = -1;

    flag = false;

    if (rolled === -1) rolled = arr[rng.int(0, arr.length - 1)]!;
    if (PRE_REDUCED.has(rolled) && rng.int(0, 2) !== 0) rolled = 0;
    if (tryGetFails(rolled, st)) { flag = true; rolled = -1; }
  }
}
````

```
839	export function runSurfaceChestsPass(st: TileStore, rng: RNG, gs: GenState, chests: ChestSink): void {
```

```
97:	public bool[] CreateBoolSet(bool defaultState, params int[] types)
	public bool[] CreateBoolSet(bool defaultState, params int[] types)
	{
		bool[] boolBuffer = GetBoolBuffer();
		for (int i = 0; i < boolBuffer.Length; i++)
		{
			boolBuffer[i] = defaultState;
		}
		for (int j = 0; j < types.Length; j++)
		{
			boolBuffer[types[j]] = !defaultState;
		}
		return boolBuffer;
	}

```
